Matcha Flavoured Wiki — English
Tsumun · Matcha 1.12.9 · Minecraft Java 26.3

========================================================================
Overview
========================================================================

Matcha Flavoured by Klei Wright and contributors. Community wiki created by Tsumun. This document covers Matcha 1.12.9 for Minecraft Java 26.3.

All published guides and catalogue references follow in one document. Explicit secrets, debug entries and hidden aliases remain excluded. Static source and browser checks do not replace in-game testing.

========================================================================
Install Matcha Flavoured
========================================================================

The datapack supplies the game rules; the resource pack supplies names and textures. Both need to be active, whether you install Matcha as a mod or as a datapack.

Choose the mod or the datapack

For Minecraft 26.3, install the compatible mod or place the datapack in your world’s datapacks folder. Do not load the mod and a second copy of the datapack together.

With the mod edition, check that its resources are active; use the matching separate resource pack if needed.

With the datapack edition, select the pack for the world and activate the matching resource pack.

Check before starting

Look for the Matcha loaded message when the world starts or reloads. Then check names and textures. The creator recommends starting a new world.

========================================================================
Your first workshop
========================================================================

Start with Kindling and a Mud Kiln. Copper tools replace Minecraft’s usual stone tools.

Make and light Kindling

Craft two sticks above two logs in a 2×2 arrangement. The result is unlit Kindling. Place it, then light it with Tinder or a Flint Firestarter.

Use a hoe to harvest grass. Dry Short Grass or Fern over the fire.

Combine one Dirt and one Short Dry Grass to make Packed Mud.

Reach copper, then iron

Build a Mud Kiln from mud bricks, packed mud and Kindling. It processes raw copper into ingots. Later, use a Blast Furnace for raw iron.

Follow advancements alongside the recipe book: their descriptions explain the intended next step.

Harvest grass for grain and fuel

Use a hoe on Short Grass: a successful grain roll gives Wheat Grain; otherwise you collect Short Grass to dry over Kindling. Shears preserve the grass directly. Hand-breaking does not use the hoe’s grain drop.

========================================================================
Advancements and progression
========================================================================

Browse the public advancement branches in Matcha Flavoured 1.12.9, with the pack’s original names, descriptions and icons.

Reading the tree

The overview shows the early stages of the main branch. Choose another branch or the full view to browse further. Hidden advancements and secret recipe categories are omitted. Open the Advancements menu in Minecraft to see your own progress.

========================================================================
Choose the right heat source
========================================================================

Matcha reuses familiar blocks with different jobs. A vanilla furnace is an Oven; a vanilla smoker is a Mud Kiln.

Fire and food

Kindling is used for early cooking and drying grass. The Oven cooks prepared dishes.

Do not assume every campfire dish also has an Oven recipe. Check the individual entry.

Materials and metals

The Mud Kiln processes early materials, including raw copper. The Blast Furnace handles raw iron and Carbon-Rich Iron. Ordinary Oven cooking is not the iron-smelting route.

In recipe viewers, a technical “smoking” category refers to the Mud Kiln.

========================================================================
Food, cooking and healing
========================================================================

Cook for healing and useful effects. Matcha’s foods use their own Regeneration effects, while the pack disables natural regeneration.

Food restores health

Natural regeneration is disabled by the pack’s initial settings. Most finished foods replace nutrition with short Regeneration. Steak provides Regeneration III for 2.4 seconds: up to 2 hearts from that initial burst, if you are injured and nothing blocks it. The healing icon and particles are often hidden.

Hunger can stop healing

Outside Easy, the Hunger effect can clear food Regeneration. The food bar still exists internally and is maintained by the pack. Bread, Honey Ginger Tea and Ice Cream cleanse the pack’s harmful-effect list. Milk clears every effect, including useful bonuses.

Choose the cooking station

Kindling has 30 food recipes, each taking 10 seconds. The Oven prepares those basics in 5 seconds. It also finishes five stocks in 15 seconds: Japanese Curry, Green Curry, Pumpkin Curry, Paneer Makhani and Tonkotsu Ramen. The Mud Kiln has four food recipes: Naan plus Charred Meat, Charred Fish and Charred Potato, all taking 10 seconds.

Ingredients and unfinished preparations

Flour, Dough, Oil, Molasses, Seitan and the stocks are ingredients or intermediate preparations. They are not interchangeable with a finished meal. Use Wheat Grain for grain; the item normally called Wheat is Straw. Dough becomes Bread over Kindling or in the Oven, and Naan in the Mud Kiln. Check each recipe’s result and next step.

Bonuses, containers and throwable jars

Compare each food’s exact level, duration and chance. Health Boost raises maximum health; Absorption is temporary protection, not restored health. Many dishes return a bowl or bottle after consumption. Sparkling Mead and pickled Crimson, Toadstool and Warped fungi are splash potions to throw. Their stored duration is a maximum that splash distance can reduce.

Raw ingredients can still be risky

Cooking changes actual item components, not just the picture. The supplied 26.3 client retains a 30% chance of Hunger I for 30 seconds on raw poultry. Raw pufferfish has Poison II for 60 seconds, plus Hunger III and Nausea I for 15 seconds. Matcha’s cooked pufferfish instead has its own healing and Conduit Power.

========================================================================
Metallurgy: from ore to equipment
========================================================================

Metallurgy in Matcha is a set of choices: process raw materials in the right furnace, make an alloy, then upgrade a compatible base at a Smithing Table. Copper branches into Hepatizon or Shakudo; iron into steel; diamond into Electrum or Adamant.

Start with the workshop

Build a Mud Kiln for the first copper, then a Blast Furnace for the broader ore-processing routes. Steel unlocks the workshop’s next two stations: a Smithing Table costs 2 Steel Alloy and 4 planks; an Anvil costs 3 Steel Alloy and 4 Iron Ingots.

The Mud Kiln needs 4 Mud Bricks, 2 Packed Mud and 1 Kindling. The Blast Furnace needs 4 Polished Deepslate, 2 Smooth Stone and 1 Kindling.

The Oven is the cooking furnace. Do not use it as a substitute for the Blast Furnace when processing iron.

Ore is not always the finished material

Ordinary ore drops include Raw Copper, Raw Iron, Raw Gold, Raw Silver and Raw Diamond. Silver ore is called Native Silver in Matcha. Raw Diamond must be processed before it becomes the diamond used in equipment recipes.

Raw Copper makes 1 Copper Ingot in 20 seconds in the Mud Kiln, or 10 seconds in the Blast Furnace. Raw Iron, Gold, Silver and Diamond each take 10 seconds in the Blast Furnace.

Silk Touch preserves compatible ore blocks. Adamant tools instead provide processed drops on the ore loot tables that support their intrinsic effect.

Make steel in batches of four

Combine 4 Iron Ingots and 1 Coal Block without a fixed arrangement to make 4 Carbon-Rich Iron. Each Carbon-Rich Iron becomes 1 Steel Alloy after 200 ticks in a Blast Furnace: 4 Steel Alloy from the full batch.

The coal block is consumed by crafting. The Blast Furnace still needs its own fuel.

A Steel Pickaxe has 3,000 durability, compared with 500 in the pack’s crafted Iron Pickaxe recipe. It is repaired with Iron Ingots.

Choose a copper branch: Hepatizon or Shakudo

Hepatizon Alloy combines 7 Copper Ingots, 1 Silver Bullion and 1 Gold Ingot. Shakudo Alloy combines 4 Copper Blocks and 2 Gold Blocks. Both upgrade copper equipment; the base is copper, not iron or diamond.

Hepatizon favours speed. Its pickaxe has a base mining-speed value of 20 and 1,000 durability; its armour also adds movement speed.

Shakudo’s pickaxe has Silk Touch I and 1,000 durability. Armour cleansing depends on the slot: helmet clears blindness and darkness; chestplate poison; leggings slowness; boots levitation.

Both families also have an Elytra upgrade. Open that exact recipe rather than assuming the armour recipe applies to wings.

Choose a diamond branch: Electrum or Adamant

Electrum Alloy costs 4 Gold Ingots, 4 Silver Bullion and 1 Pith. Adamant Alloy costs 4 Adamant Scrap, 4 Gold Ingots and 1 Pith. Both require diamond equipment as the upgrade base. They are parallel options, not consecutive smithing steps.

Crafting 1 Amalgamated Pith yields 9 Pith. Matcha repurposes these item identities; use their Matcha names and textures rather than a vanilla recipe guide.

Electrum’s pickaxe has the pack’s harvesting bonus III, supported by specific loot tables. This is separate from the ordinary Fortune enchantment, which is capped at I.

Adamant’s pickaxe starts with Efficiency II, Unbreaking II and automatic processing of supported drops. Its base durability is 5,000; the Electrum Pickaxe has 3,000.

Upgrade once, repair with the correct material

Place the compatible equipment and 1 alloy in a Smithing Table. The standard pickaxe upgrades shown below do not require a smithing template. Specialized Dolabra, Mattock and Claymore recipes are different: check their template slot. Repairing later uses the equipment’s repair material, which can be different from the alloy used to make it.

Hepatizon and Shakudo pickaxes: Copper Block. Steel Pickaxe: Iron Ingot. Electrum Pickaxe: Diamond Block. Adamant Pickaxe: Diamond Block or Adamant Scrap.

The pack resets the prior-work repair-cost component on inventory updates. This avoids accumulating that penalty; it does not make repair materials free or turn an alloy into a universal repair item.

Craft the template for specialized equipment

The five alloy families include Dolabra and Mattock recipes; steel and Adamant also have a Claymore recipe. These 12 specialized recipes consume a smithing template as well as the base equipment and alloy. Standard pickaxe recipes do not.

Craft 1 template from 7 Iron Ingots, 1 Steel Alloy and 1 Deepslate, Cobbled Deepslate or Blackstone. The three stones are alternatives for one ingredient slot.

A Dolabra uses an axe as its base; a Mattock uses a hoe; a Claymore uses a sword. Follow the recipe rather than combining two tools yourself.

Process bulk ore and recycle deliberately

A Raw Copper, Iron or Gold Block becomes its metal block in 400 ticks in a Blast Furnace. A block represents 9 units: bulk processing saves furnace time per unit without adding extra metal.

The pack also lists equipment-recycling recipes. They consume the entire selected item and return the recipe’s fixed output, not all the materials originally used to craft it.

Check the exact recipe before recycling upgraded or enchanted equipment: internal item identities can be shared between different Matcha pieces.

========================================================================
Build with the Stonecutter
========================================================================

The Stonecutter works on much more than stone. Matcha adds cutting routes for wood, glass, wool and metals.

Compare the output

Look up the cutting recipe before committing a large stack. In 1.12.9, one Glass makes four Glass Panes, and one Copper Ingot makes six Copper Chains.

Wood and wool also have their own cutting conversions; use the material name to find them.

Recover building materials

Some recipes let you turn slabs back into full blocks. For example, two Acacia Slabs make one block of Acacia Planks.

========================================================================
Fish by habitat
========================================================================

Fishing depends on the region. The pack separates freshwater and saltwater catches, with additional climate and special-biome groups.

Change location to change catches

Use the Angler’s Almanac and fish descriptions to choose a habitat. Carp are associated with temperate freshwater; Herring with cold saltwater.

Moving between a river and an ocean can matter more than fishing longer in the same spot.

A single species can list more than one suitable habitat.

Fishing stars

The 47 rated fish have one to four stars. The rating appears in each fish’s item entry, together with its habitats. Open the Rarity and stars guide for the full list and use the item index’s Rarity filter to browse a tier.

Use your catch

Caught fish can support cooking and trade. The fishing advancement track also records discoveries and distinguishes fish, junk and treasure.

The Fishmonger advancement directs players to sell caught fish to a Fisherman.

Choose the biome, not just the water

The fishing location’s biome selects the habitat. A handmade pool in Plains uses temperate freshwater catches; one in Taiga uses cool freshwater catches. The water’s appearance does not determine the species.

Deserts, Badlands and Savannas use hot/dry freshwater; Jungles use hot/wet freshwater.

Ocean and Beach use temperate saltwater; Warm Ocean uses hot saltwater.

Lush and Dripstone Caves use temperate freshwater. Deep Dark has its own Echo Fish, Sculk Vein and Echo Shard table.

Sulfur Caves have a special habitat table containing Fish Bones or three Sulfur Chunks. An unsupported biome without a matching fallback produces junk on its habitat roll.

Treasure needs open water

The overall catch first selects junk, treasure or a habitat result. The treasure branch requires the fishing hook to qualify as being in open water. A fish’s stars are not a universal catch percentage: luck, open water and the selected habitat also matter.

========================================================================
Rarity and stars
========================================================================

Fish caught with a rod have a rating from one to four stars. Some other Matcha items use a coloured name to show their rarity.

Reading fishing stars

The stars belong to the species you catch. The pack defines four tiers: grey for one star, green for two, cyan for three and purple for four. The item index shows this rating for all 47 rated fish.

The rating comes from the catch’s tooltip. Cooked fish have their own item entries.

Use the Rarity filter in the item index to browse one fishing tier at a time.

Habitat and catch chances

The available species depend on the fishing location: freshwater, saltwater, climate and special areas such as the Deep Dark. A star rating does not give a universal catch percentage. The habitat’s loot table and the selection of fish, treasure or junk also affect the result.

Item-name colours

Matcha also assigns Minecraft’s name-colour rarity to selected items: common, uncommon, rare or epic. For example, the Crystal Heart is rare and The Lesser Key of Solomon is epic. The item index records the values explicitly set by this pack.

The fishing stars and item-name colours use separate scales. A two-star fish has a green fishing rating; an uncommon item has a yellow name.

Entries without an explicit Matcha value are left unlabelled. Enchantments and server changes can alter an item’s appearance in game.

========================================================================
Troubleshoot names and recipes
========================================================================

If a recipe fails, check the installed versions, active packs and workstation first.

Wrong textures or translation keys

Names such as item.kleispack… or unexpected vanilla appearances usually point to missing or mismatched resources. Activate the matching resource pack and check its priority.

A different resource pack placed above Matcha may override its names or visuals.

The craft still fails

Make sure only one copy of Matcha’s gameplay data is loaded. Then compare your workstation, ingredients and arrangement with the recipe for your version.

Server datapacks or other mods may change the result. A standalone wiki cannot inspect those server changes.

For a bug report, include the Minecraft and Matcha versions, station used and exact ingredients.

========================================================================
Health, death and maximum hearts
========================================================================

Matcha removes ordinary hunger upkeep and ties recovery to food and effects. Maximum hearts, death penalties and difficulty form a separate progression system.

Hunger and healing

The pack maintains hunger with saturation and disables natural health regeneration during its initial rule setup. With the matching resource pack active, transparent hunger icons hide the hunger bar. Being well-fed does not provide vanilla-style automatic healing. Cooked food can restore health through effects; cooked steak, for example, applies regeneration.

Current health and maximum health are different: healing does not automatically restore a lost maximum heart.

Keep Inventory and death

Keep Inventory is enabled by Matcha’s initial world setup. Under that configuration, death retains inventory, but it still affects maximum health. The death handler removes one maximum heart through Normal difficulty, or two in Hard, before applying the active lower limit.

At the active floor, a death cannot reduce maximum health further through this handler.

These are the bundled defaults. A server’s game rules, plugins or dimension handling can change the observed inventory behaviour.

The heart floor changes with progression

The heart system has a cap of 30 hearts. Easy uses a floor of 10 hearts. Normal and Hard use a personal progression floor, initialized to 10 hearts and reduced by certain milestones, down to a minimum of 3.

When a milestone takes that personal floor below 6 hearts while the world is Normal, the pack changes the world difficulty to Hard. This affects the world, not only that player.

The floor is a lower bound for maximum health, not an immediate damage amount or a guarantee of full current health.

Recover maximum hearts

An accepted Crystal Heart activation adds one maximum heart, without exceeding the cap. Activation is refused at the cap, during its short cooldown, or while a Totem of Undying is held in the main hand. The pack then updates the player’s maximum-health attribute.

Wither can reduce maximum health

In Normal and Hard difficulty, the Wither status effect can reduce your maximum health if it lasts long enough. Its warning meter is separate from ordinary Wither damage. Remove the effect before the meter runs out; Shakudō equipment can give you more time. The loss respects your current minimum-health floor.

Raw Estus and Estus Ashes

Some undead drop raw Estus. Outside Creative mode, inventory collection converts one unit into Estus Ashes and applies temporary healing and protection. You do not eat the raw Estus.

Easy or lower: Regeneration V for 4 seconds and Resistance I for 10 seconds. Normal or Hard: Regeneration V for 2 seconds and Resistance I for 5 seconds.

These are temporary effects, not guaranteed instant healing. Hunger can interfere with Regeneration under the conditions explained in the cooking guide.

========================================================================
Cold water, time and sleep
========================================================================

The environment changes several survival rules directly: water can be dangerous in frozen biomes, the day cycle is longer, and sleep uses a custom time-acceleration system.

Freezing water is a conditional hazard

Cold water in frozen biomes slows you, darkens your vision and deals freezing damage. Frost Protection on your chest armour prevents this cold-water effect. You can be affected without being fully underwater.

Without the relevant protection, the handler repeatedly applies Slowness, Darkness and freezing damage. It is not restricted to being fully submerged.

Frost Protection on the chest armour blocks this handler. The predicate checks for the enchantment’s presence, with no minimum level specified.

The Darkness status effect from cold water is separate from the world’s night lighting.

A longer day and night

The day timeline lasts 72,000 game ticks: a complete cycle takes about 60 minutes at 20 ticks per second, before sleep or commands advance the clock. Night uses custom sky-light, sky-colour and fog values. Shaders and visual settings can still alter how those values look.

Sleep accelerates time

Overworld beds allow sleep during the day as well as at night. Matcha replaces the usual instant night skip with a custom clock: its acceleration is calculated from the proportion of sleeping players relative to players in the Overworld.

The pack calculates a sleep duration and wakes sleepers when it expires. Completing sleep also clears the weather.

The pack disables phantom spawning during initial rule setup.

========================================================================
Enchanting, books and levels
========================================================================

Matcha uses crafted Blessings and traded Ofuda to give equipment specific enchantments. Check the book, its level and the equipment it supports.

Craft a Blessing

Prepare a Hell-Bound Book, then choose one of the 23 Blessing recipes. Each recipe produces a fixed set of enchantments at fixed levels. The wiki uses the prayer or elegy title to distinguish books that are all named Blessing in game.

Trade for an Ofuda

Five Ofuda are obtained from the Mouthpiece profession, which replaces the Librarian. Bring the required treasure book and the indicated number of Crystal Heart items. The five prayers have distinct effects even though they share the same item model.

Apply a book with an anvil

Use the anvil to apply the book’s compatible enchantments to equipment. The reference lists the effects, maximum level, supported equipment and known exclusions. Built-in alloy effects are also listed on equipment entries.

Enchanting tables and loot books

The enchanting table’s selection is restricted to curses. Fishing and chest books follow their own loot definitions: many use a random-loot pool, while others specify an enchantment or an explicit list. The rare ominous trial-chamber reward uses the table’s selection.

Levels and Mending

Matcha sets a player’s level to 10,000 during the initial item-version update and on the configured anvil interactions. This is a grant at those moments, not a permanent level lock. Mending still repairs through XP collection; its pack definition declares a repair multiplier of two. The supplied levels alone do not prove automatic repair.

Treasure books are a separate item type

The Avesta, Quran, Tanakh, Book of Enoch and Lesser Key of Solomon are treasure books used for trades. They do not contain stored enchantments themselves. Paradise Lost and The Divine Comedy are literary treasures that can also be requested by Cartographers.

========================================================================
What changes in the Nether
========================================================================

Matcha changes how you gather Nether resources and allows Crying Obsidian in portal frames. Some familiar blocks also have new names and drops.

Build the portal with the right blocks

A Nether portal frame can use Obsidian, Crying Obsidian, or a mixture of both. This expands the usable frame materials; it does not replace the portal with a new type of dimension.

Look for resources between structures

Hellspores are the pack’s Nether Wart. Matcha adds natural patches in Nether Wastes and Soul Sand Valleys, so explore the landscape as well as structures. The usual Nether gravel deposits are removed from the biomes that generated them.

Sulfurous Hellstone replaces the Nether Quartz Ore identity. Its normal mined drop is Sulfur Chunk; Silk Touch preserves the block.

Do not rely on bucket water below the roof

The pack has a specific rule for water placed with a bucket between Y=0 and Y=127 in the Nether: it removes water around the placing player. Do not plan a lava escape or a fire defence around that water source.

========================================================================
What changes in the End
========================================================================

The End keeps its familiar exploration landmarks. Matcha’s confirmed changes here concern access, useful drops and the effect of defeating the Ender Dragon.

Check the new Eye of Ender recipe

Eyes of Ender use Stabilized Estus and Pith in Matcha, rather than the vanilla Ender Pearl and Blaze Powder combination. Check that recipe before planning a resource-gathering trip. The pack has its own End advancement tab.

Recognise the changed drops

The Ender Pearl item is named Stable Void in Matcha. Endermen killed by a player drop it. You use it to craft Stabilized Estus, which then becomes an ingredient in Eyes of Ender.

A player-killed Shulker can drop a complete Sack, the pack’s Shulker Box, instead of a shell. This drop is not guaranteed.

What the Dragon victory changes

The Dragon-kill advancement awards Amalgamated Pith and switches on a safer-surface spawn rule in the Overworld. For the affected ordinary hostile mobs, subsequent spawn checks reject exposed-sky locations and above-sea-level locations outside dungeons.

This is not a global removal of existing mobs. Caves and dungeons still require preparation, and the rule is scoped to the Overworld.

From Benzene to 16 Eyes of Ender

Stable Void still matters for reaching the End: it is used in Stabilized Estus. The final Eye recipe takes Stabilized Estus and Pith, while the preparation chain also needs supplies from the Nether and Estus Ash.

Make one Benzene from one Glass Bottle, three Hellspores and one Sulfur Chunk in a shapeless recipe.

Make one Stabilized Estus from seven Estus Ash, one Benzene and one Stable Void. Follow the shaped recipe shown below.

Combine one Stabilized Estus and one Pith to produce 16 Eyes of Ender.

Repair and upgrade Elytra

Matcha prepares plain, unenchanted Elytra for repair with Honeycomb or Feathers when equipped. Plain Elytra also serve as the base for Hepatizon and Shakudo Elytra. Check the specific smithing recipe before spending an alloy.

========================================================================
Creatures and encounters
========================================================================

Matcha changes creature spawns, attributes, equipment and loot. The changes depend on the creature and difficulty; some enemies have less health but behave differently.

Combat changes by creature

In Normal and Hard, regular adult zombies move faster and step up full blocks. Husks gain health, armour and knockback resistance. Common skeleton variants receive bows with Punch, while spiders gain stronger jumps, Weaving and immunity to fall damage.

Creepers, cave spiders, baby zombies and silverfish have their own reduced maximum-health settings. Difficulty does not mean the same change for every enemy.

In Nether fortresses, nearby wither skeletons can trigger block destruction in front of them. A wall or pillar is not always equivalent to its base-Minecraft use.

Where common hostiles appear

The Iconoclast advancement, earned by killing an Ender Dragon, enables a stricter surface rule in the Overworld. This changes how the pack checks its group of common hostiles.

Before Iconoclast, checked enemies that are not undead are removed when exposed to the sky. Creepers also have a surface restriction; undead can still be encountered outdoors.

After Iconoclast, the check also uses a surface-height range, with a dungeon exception for that height test. Drowned receive an additional check. Covered areas, underground areas and creatures from other packs are not automatically safe.

Loot is also reworked

Several rewritten loot pools require a player kill, including those used by zombies, skeletons, husks and endermen. Automatic killing is therefore not interchangeable with a player kill.

Use Matcha item names when checking loot: Ender Pearls are Stable Void, and turtle meat uses the Raw Flesh entry. The underlying Minecraft identifier can be misleading.

Where can you summon the Wither?

Summon the Wither on the Overworld surface, with open sky and at Y=63 or higher. Matcha rejects underground summons and summons in the Nether or End, removing the newly summoned Wither.

Adult zombies

Hostile

In Normal and Hard, regular adult zombies move faster and can step up a full block. This rule does not apply to every zombie-family entity.

Their maximum health is set to 10 HP in Normal and 15 HP in Hard. Hard also sets a higher base attack value.

Adult husks

Hostile

Normal and Hard make them more durable through health and armour, with high knockback resistance and greater reach when tracking a target.

Normal: 30 HP and 12 armour points. Hard: 40 HP and 14 armour points.

Skeletons and variants

Hostile

Skeletons, strays, bogged and parched are covered by the common-hostile skeleton check. Their bows receive Punch I in Normal and Punch II in Hard.

Their maximum health is 10 HP in Easy/Normal and 12 HP in Hard.

Creepers

Hostile

The spawn rules set maximum health to 14/16/18 HP in Easy/Normal/Hard. They are not simply given more health than in base Minecraft.

Spiders

Hostile

In Normal and Hard, they jump higher and take no fall damage. They receive Weaving I in Normal and Weaving II in Hard.

Cave spiders

Hostile

Their maximum health is 2/4/6 HP in Easy/Normal/Hard. Their movement speed also increases with difficulty.

Baby zombie-family mobs

Hostile

The active baby-zombie checks set maximum health to 4 HP in Easy and Normal, and 7 HP in Hard.

Silverfish

Hostile

The pack sets maximum health to 2 HP in Easy/Normal and 4 HP in Hard.

Wither skeletons

Hostile

In a Nether fortress, Wither Skeletons within five blocks of a player can destroy two blocks directly in front of their eyes.

Endermen

Neutral; retaliates

Their rewritten drop table requires a player kill for Stable Void, the item used in place of the ordinary Ender Pearl.

Passive animals

Passive

Loot changes also affect peaceful animals. The turtle table, for example, supplies Raw Flesh and supports cooking-on-death conditions.

Maximum health set by the spawn rules

Base maximum-health values, before equipment and effects. A dash means no specific value is set for that difficulty. Two HP equal one heart.

Adult zombies

Easy: — · Normal: 10 · Hard: 15

Adult husks

Easy: — · Normal: 30 · Hard: 40

Skeletons and variants

Easy: 10 · Normal: 10 · Hard: 12

Creepers

Easy: 14 · Normal: 16 · Hard: 18

Cave spiders

Easy: 2 · Normal: 4 · Hard: 6

Baby zombie-family mobs

Easy: 4 · Normal: 4 · Hard: 7

Silverfish

Easy: 2 · Normal: 2 · Hard: 4

========================================================================
Villagers and trading
========================================================================

Bring villagers to your settlement, understand their food requests and check the cost of changing a profession.

Call a trader and recruit villagers

In the Overworld, Beacon Kindling signals a Wandering Trader. Keep its fire lit and remain nearby while you wait: the timer is designed to bring the trader after about ten minutes, with a visit of about five minutes. Extinguishing or breaking the fire ends the visit. The trader offers Asylum Seekers: use one on the ground to bring that villager into your settlement.

Food requests and Amnestics

A working villager who has gained trading experience can receive an individual food request. Fishermen and unemployed villagers are excluded. Check the exact dish shown in the offer. To reset a villager’s profession, use an Amnestic directly on them. This resets their profession, level and trading experience, and makes their food preference eligible to be chosen again. Consuming the Amnestic yourself instead clears your recipe-book unlocks.

Warding Stones defend a limited area

A Warding Stone can help defend an Overworld base. It gives regeneration to players and selected friendly creatures within 16 blocks, slows selected enemies within 24 blocks, and damages one eligible target at a time. It does not cover every hostile mob: creepers and spiders are not among its listed targets. Do not place one in a Trial Chamber or an Overgrown Abbey; the placement is rejected with an explosion.

Check the requested dish and quantity

After gaining trading experience, an eligible villager can receive one of 42 favorite-food requests for one emerald. Most ask for one dish, but the four dinosaur-nugget requests need eight nuggets, Puerquito needs four, and Honey Ginger Tea needs three. Each offer has one use before restocking.

The pack checks eligible villagers every 30 seconds. A request may take time to appear after the first trade. Fishermen and unemployed villagers are excluded.

Keep the signal loaded and allow for its cooldown

Beacon Kindling gives its user a personal 15-minute cooldown. Keep its area loaded and its fire lit while waiting for the trader. Extinguishing or breaking the fire ends the visit but does not reset that cooldown.

========================================================================
Crops and livestock
========================================================================

Wheat grain, straw, tomatoes and livestock have distinct roles in Matcha.

Wheat grain, straw and tomatoes

Matcha separates Wheat Grain from Straw. Wheat Grain is both the seed you replant and an ingredient, while mature wheat also provides Straw. Harvest short grass with a hoe to have a chance of finding grain; ordinary hand-breaking does not use that seed drop. Tomatoes replace vanilla beetroot, and one tomato can be crafted into three Tomato Seeds. Keep part of your harvest for replanting.

Buy livestock from farmers

Farmers can help you start a herd. Their level-two offers are drawn from fertilized eggs; level-three offers are drawn from calves and piglets. Which variants are available depends on the biome where the trader stands. Chickens also drop a feather when laying their normal variant’s egg, so feathers do not always require killing a bird.

Calling a Happy Ghast

Play a Goat Horn to teleport one eligible Happy Ghast within 80 blocks to three blocks in front of you. The pack does not guarantee that the nearest one is selected.

Start four crops with a Seed Bundle

A Farmer’s Seed Bundle contains 16 Wheat Grain, 16 Tomato Seeds, four Carrots and four Potatoes. The Exotic Seed Bundle instead holds 16 Melon Seeds, 16 Pumpkin Seeds, eight Sweet Berries and eight Glow Berries. Remove their contents like those of an ordinary bundle.

Use purchased animals on ordinary ground

Place purchased animals and fertilized eggs on ordinary ground. Using an egg on a Spawner or Trial Spawner triggers a different rule: outside Creative, Matcha destroys nearby spawner blocks and hurts the user instead of supporting a spawner conversion.

========================================================================
Maps and archaeology
========================================================================

Find exploration maps and understand the rewards available at archaeological sites.

Maps and archaeology guide exploration

Wandering Traders can sell maps to villages and witch huts. Their map offers are selected from a shared list, so a particular destination is not guaranteed. Bring a Brush when exploring ruins: Matcha replaces several archaeology loot tables. Trail Ruins, for example, can provide seeds and building supplies in common finds, alongside a separate rare-find pool. A Brush does not reveal a fixed reward at every site.

Choose ruins for their possible finds

Cold and warm ocean ruins can yield a Rusty Axe or Trident; warm ruins can also yield a Sniffer Egg. Desert wells can provide the Avesta or a clay fetish. Rare Trail Ruins finds include Amber, Hepatizon Alloy and clay fetishes alongside decorative treasures. These are loot-pool possibilities, not a fixed reward at every site.

Clay fetishes change the weather

The two Clay Fetishes share an item name but have different roles. Use the cheerful variant to schedule clear weather, or the mournful variant to schedule rain, after a three-second delay. Open each entry to distinguish the two variants.

1 × Block of Iron · Blast Furnace · 20 s

Block of Raw Gold

1 × Block of Gold · Blast Furnace · 20 s

Hepatizon Alloy

Craft 7 Copper Ingots, 1 Silver Bullion and 1 Gold Ingot into 1 alloy.

Speed: fast mining tools, movement bonuses on armour and some held tools. Choose the individual piece for its exact attributes.

Repair materials for the example pickaxe: Block of Copper

Shakudo Alloy

Craft 4 Copper Blocks and 2 Gold Blocks into 1 alloy: blocks, not ingots.

Silk Touch on mining tools; armour combines cleansing and magic protection. A sword instead has its own combat effects.

Repair materials for the example pickaxe: Block of Copper

Steel Alloy

4 Iron Ingots + 1 Coal Block make 4 Carbon-Rich Iron. Blast each piece for 10 seconds to obtain 4 Steel Alloy in total.

Durability: the pickaxe has 3,000 durability. Steel swords add attack knockback; armour adds knockback resistance.

Repair materials for the example pickaxe: Iron Ingot

Electrum Alloy

Craft 4 Gold Ingots, 4 Silver Bullion and 1 Pith into 1 alloy.

Harvesting bonuses on supported drops. Armour contributes to Warding; the sword has Looting II, Smite III and Warding III.

Repair materials for the example pickaxe: Block of Diamond

Adamant Alloy

Craft 4 Adamant Scrap, 4 Gold Ingots and 1 Pith into 1 alloy.

Tools automatically process supported drops. The pickaxe starts with Efficiency II and Unbreaking II; armour and weapons have different intrinsic effects.

Repair materials for the example pickaxe: Block of Diamond, Adamant Scrap

Mining speed is a component value, not blocks per second. Recipe durations assume 20 ticks per second. Repair materials below are specific to the listed item.

========================================================================
Books and enchantment effects
========================================================================

The enchanting-table pool is restricted to curses. Loot books use several different rules: random-loot pools, fixed enchantments or explicit lists. The ominous rare trial-chamber reward uses the enchanting-table pool.

23 crafted Blessings have distinct models and lore but share a generic gameName. Five traded Ofuda share one model and must stay distinct by lore and stored enchantments.

Blessing recipe keys require minecraft:enchanted_book by ID, without a particular stored curse or lore.

Flagged intrinsic equipment merges active and stored enchantments by the higher level, then removes stored_enchantments and clears the flag. Explicit cleanups remove Silk Touch for intrinsic Electrum Tool, Sharpness for intrinsic Smite, and Fortune for intrinsic Silk Touch or Electrum Tool.

Missing direct effects does not mean inactive: Fortune, Electrum Tool and Adamant Tool are explicitly read by block loot tables.

Warding runs through tick -> stopwatches -> timer -> on_restart. Effective power is max(min(held equipment total,3), Electrum armour count). Powers I–II run each one-second cycle and III–IV each half-second cycle. Held Warding IV is therefore capped to effective III; four Electrum armour pieces can produce effective IV.

Powers1–3 can return before damage when the nearby copper-armour branch fires; its resistance helper only shows particles/sound. Power4 instead has a copper damage branch. Preserve target tags and selectors rather than promising universal undead protection.

The initial cooldown depends on power. However apply returns immediately after giving regeneration for power>=1, before its final cooldown-reset call; do not publish a fixed repeated interval without an in-game check.

Any Frost Protection on chest armour bypasses the freezing-water handler. Its executable predicate does not require levelIII despite a comment saying maximum.

Derived loss-removal probabilities:2L/(5L+5) on armour andL/(L+1) otherwise. These are probabilities, not guaranteed durability multipliers.

The pack supplies XP at initialization and qualifying anvil interactions. Engine book-application and combination rules are not implemented by these archives; universal item compatibility is not claimed.

Four Adamant armour pieces or four Electrum pieces call Divinity from the tick loop. It refreshes Absorption I for 31 seconds when its 600-tick cooldown reaches zero.

Adamant armour

Claymore, Steel Dolabra, Steel Helmet, Steel Hoe, Steel Leggings, Steel Mattock, Steel Pickaxe, Steel Shears, Steel Shovel, Steel Spear, Steel Sword, Sturdy Leather Boots, Sturdy Leather Gambeson, Sturdy Leather Helmet, Sturdy Leather Leggings, Tinder, Trident, Warding Shield, Warding Sword, Wooden Axe, Wooden Hoe, Wooden Pickaxe, Wooden Shovel, Wooden Spear, Wooden Sword

Wind Burst

Creates a wind burst on a qualifying falling attack. Higher levels produce stronger knockback.

Maximum level: 3

Compatible equipment: Mace

Requires at least 1.5 blocks of falling distance and does not trigger while flying.

========================================================================
Food and healing reference
========================================================================

First-load setup disables natural_health_regeneration. Ordinary nutrition cannot naturally heal while this rule remains disabled.

Hunger remains internal: each tick, the pack gives Saturation II for 1 second when the food score is 8 or lower. The code uses execute at @a then @p, so precise multiplayer targeting has not been tested. All six supplied food-bar HUD sprites are fully transparent (alpha 0), confirming that the resource pack hides the bar.

When the Regeneration effects_changed advancement fires, it clears Regeneration if Hunger is active and the stored difficulty score is not Easy (1). This can cancel food healing. The Hunger countdown function only displays UI; it is not the blocking mechanism.

The supplied 26.3 client defaults apply_effects probability to 1 and consume_seconds to 1.6. Consumable.canConsume permits consumption when FOOD is absent. Thus !minecraft:food does not make a custom consumable inedible.

Regeneration heals 1 HP at intervals of 50 >> amplifier ticks in this client. A finite isolated effect gives floor(duration / interval) HP while health is missing. Two HP equal one heart. Existing effects, full health and Hunger can change the actual result.

Several meals apply short strong and long weak Regeneration entries. The client stores weaker longer effects as hidden effects and counts their duration down during the stronger one. Their durations and HP totals are not independent simultaneous streams.

Bread, Honey Ginger Tea and Ice Cream remove #matcha:harmful. Milk Bottle clears all effects, including beneficial ones. Honey Bottle retains only Poison removal from the supplied client.

Cleansing recipes list healing apply_effects before remove_effects. With Hunger already active, the advancement may clear the heal before Hunger is removed. Event order needs an in-game check; do not promise that one bite both clears Hunger and fully heals.

Four culinary jars are splash potions, with hidden potion_contents: Sparkling Mead (Speed II, 300 s), Pickled Crimson Fungus (Weakness II, 60 s), Pickled Toadstools (Poison II, 60 s), Pickled Warped Fungus (Invisibility I, 300 s). These are stored maxima; the client scales duration by splash distance.

Public food recipes comprise 30 campfire recipes at 200 ticks, 35 Oven recipes (30 at 100 ticks and 5 at 300 ticks), and 4 Mud Kiln recipes at 200 ticks. The Blast Furnace has no recipe under the public food directory. Other stations can still transform non-food ingredients.

Alaska Blackfish

No healing effect.

Takes 1.6 s to consume.

Cooking routes: Cooked Fish (Kindling, 10 s); Charred Fish (Mud Kiln, 10 s); Cooked Fish (Oven, 5 s).

Fish species in the same group share their cooking recipes.

Apple Empanada

Initial healing: up to 4 hearts over 4.8 s (Regeneration III).

Regeneration III — 4.8 s

Regeneration I — 180 s

Also applies a longer, weaker Regeneration. Durations start on consumption and are not independent simultaneous healing streams.

Outside Easy, Hunger can clear this Regeneration; nominal healing is not guaranteed while Hunger is active.

Takes 1.6 s to consume.

Can be consumed regardless of the hidden food bar.

The short healing entry is configured without an icon or particles; merged effects can alter the final visible icon.

Cake

Cake use applies Instant Health I (4 HP / 2 hearts for an ordinary player).

Instant Health I

Health Boost II — 180 s

Place the cake and eat a slice; the source trigger targets #matcha:cakes blocks.

Health Boost II lasts 180 seconds and raises maximum health; it is distinct from restored current health.

Lore displays four red heart glyphs, but the function applies Instant Health I. Do not derive numerical healing from lore alone.

The 4-HP value is verified in this client’s HealOrHarmMobEffect; it disagrees with treating four lore glyphs as four restored hearts.

Bedrock Buster

Explosive charge that can remove nearby bedrock, trial spawners and vaults.

Places a glowing TNT charge with a nominal four-second fuse. Its special effect removes the listed blocks in a nearby area, in addition to the TNT explosion. Removed blocks are not guaranteed to drop as items.

Warding Stone

A placeable defence that heals selected allies and attacks selected nearby enemies.

In the Overworld: regeneration within 16 blocks, selected enemies slowed within 24, and one eligible target attacked at a time.

Creepers and spiders are not listed targets. Placing it in a Trial Chamber or Overgrown Abbey triggers an explosion.

Name in game: Warding Stone

Titanium Compass

A navigation compass with a titanium appearance. Its needle follows the world spawn in the Overworld, or its linked lodestone when a tracker is present.

Available through the weighted compass loot pool used by shipwreck map chests, stronghold libraries and the Archaeologist’s Hero of the Village gifts.

Without a lodestone link, the model has no navigation target outside the Overworld. No additional tracking ability is defined in the checked item components.

Name in game: Titanium Compass

Tallow

Crafting fat found in igloo chests. It can replace honeycomb in the candle recipe.

The loot uses the honeycomb item ID; the checked candle recipe accepts that ID without a model restriction.

Name in game: Tallow

Fox Pelt

Hide dropped by a red fox, usable to craft bundles, item frames and leather armour.

Its rabbit-hide item ID is accepted by Matcha’s bundle, item-frame and leather-armour recipes.

Name in game: Fox Pelt

Fox Pelt

White hide dropped by a snowy fox. It has the same checked crafting uses as the red Fox Pelt.

Its rabbit-hide item ID is accepted by Matcha’s bundle, item-frame and leather-armour recipes.

Name in game: Fox Pelt

Sulfur Goo

A slime-like material dropped by a Sulfur Cube killed by a player. It is usable in sticky-piston and slime-block crafting.

The material retains the slime-ball item ID. These two client recipes remain available under the pack’s resource filter.

The vanilla magma-cream recipe is blocked by Matcha and is not included among the verified uses.

Name in game: Sulfur Goo

Axolotl

A live axolotl catch stored as a spawn item, allowing it to be released into the world.

The junk-fishing entry is restricted to Lush Caves and Dripstone Caves; its entity data explicitly specifies an axolotl.

In Survival, using this spawn item on a spawner destroys nearby spawners and damages the player. Release the creature away from spawners.

Name in game: Axolotl

Frog Spawn Egg

A live frog caught while fishing in swamps or mangrove swamps, usable to release a frog into the world.

The fishing entry names it Frog and limits its stack to one.

In Survival, using this spawn item on a spawner destroys nearby spawners and damages the player. Release the creature away from spawners.

Tadpole Spawn Egg

A live tadpole caught while fishing in swamps or mangrove swamps, usable to release a tadpole into the world.

The fishing entry names it Tadpole and allows stacks of up to eight.

In Survival, using this spawn item on a spawner destroys nearby spawners and damages the player. Release the creature away from spawners.

Fertilised white egg

A fertilised egg that releases a temperate chicken chick. It can be given by a Farmer to a Hero of the Village.

This catalogue identity uses the white-egg model on a chicken spawn item, with a baby temperate chicken in its entity data.

In Survival, using this spawn item on a spawner destroys nearby spawners and damages the player. Release the creature away from spawners.

========================================================================
Roadmap
========================================================================

The wiki is maintained by Tsumun. This roadmap records the next improvements without announcing a release date.

Explain items and their uses

This edition

Distinct prayer titles, enchantment effects, food properties, trade offers and source references, in English and French.

Compare source data with play sessions

Next checks

Confirm conditional mechanics and equipment behaviour in game, then follow changes between pack releases. Keep the version and verification limits visible.

Community corrections

Later · not implemented

Let readers propose a correction with its source and affected pack version. Review proposals before publication and keep English and French entries consistent. The contribution system will be designed separately.

========================================================================
Sources and credits
========================================================================

# Attributions

## Nouveau wiki / New wiki

**Nouveau wiki réalisé par Tsumun.**

Cette nouvelle version bilingue du wiki a été créée par **Tsumun**.
Les crédits ci-dessous concernent le pack original et les sources réutilisées.

**New wiki created by Tsumun.**

This new bilingual wiki was created by **Tsumun**. The notices below credit
the original pack and the reused source material.

## Community source / Source communautaire

Item identities, texture references and effect metadata were cross-checked
with [Evansch0's Field Wiki](https://github.com/Evansch0/MatchaFlavouredWiki).
Recipes, categories and the advancement diagram in this edition are assembled
from the pinned official release files and the credited source data.

## Upstream notices / Crédits des sources

The Matcha Flavoured Field Wiki is an unofficial community companion. It
reorganizes recipe data, release notes, names, models, and textures so players
can explore the pack in a browser. It is not affiliated with or endorsed by
Mojang, Microsoft, Modrinth, or any of the people credited below.

## Matcha Flavoured

- **Klei** created Matcha Flavoured and, according to the credits bundled with
  release 1.03, made the pack's textures and data except where otherwise noted.
  The original project is available on
  [Modrinth](https://modrinth.com/datapack/matcha-flavoured).
- **Ciren** composed the “Golden” music disc. The pack notes that the disc was
  added in memory of Dakota.
- **DeBlezyBestie** is credited for “Elegy of The Abbey” in the official
  1.12.9 jukebox song definition. The wiki displays its disc sprite and track
  metadata; it does not distribute or play the audio.

Matcha Flavoured is distributed under
[CC BY-NC-SA 4.0](https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-nc-sa/4.0/). This wiki
is a transformed presentation of that material: it extracts and reorganizes
pack data, generates searchable entries, and presents recipes and textures in a
new interface.

## Credits carried forward from the pack

The original pack credits thank the following people and projects for
inspiration or technical frameworks. The credits also make clear that these
people do not know of or endorse Matcha Flavoured.

- **Raspmary**: the
  [Raspberry Flavoured](https://www.curseforge.com/minecraft/modpacks/raspberry-flavoured)
  modpack inspired parts of Matcha Flavoured's progression, resources, and
  alloys.
- **JermsyBoy**: the
  [A Little Taste of Jerm](https://www.curseforge.com/minecraft/texture-packs/a-little-taste-of-jerm)
  resource pack inspired Matcha Flavoured's visual direction. JermsyBoy's
  [YouTube channel](https://www.youtube.com/channel/UCICnSwa3x5sHTWmTP6YO30w)
  is also linked in the pack credits.
- **MiziraGamez**: the
  [Repair Cost Remover](https://modrinth.com/datapack/repair-cost-remover)
  datapack provided a framework for endless repairing.
- **Gooporeal**: the
  [Gooporeal's Lichen](https://modrinth.com/resourcepack/gooporeals-lichen)
  resource pack inspired the lichen remodels.
- **Vectorwing**: the
  [Detail Brush](https://www.curseforge.com/minecraft/texture-packs/detail-brush)
  resource pack inspired many textures.
- **Rhythmic and PurpurMC**: the
  [Looting Shears](https://modrinth.com/datapack/purpurpacks-looting-shears)
  datapack provided a framework for looting shears.

Minecraft, its names, and vanilla game assets are the property of Mojang
Studios and Microsoft. Any vanilla material displayed by this wiki remains
subject to its original terms and is not relicensed under this repository's
Creative Commons license. This project is not an official Minecraft product or
service.

See the [Minecraft Usage Guidelines](https://www.minecraft.net/usage-guidelines)
for Mojang's current terms.
